### Page 1

日期：2026年6月12日分類：大會紀要 / 深度研究內部限制查閱
20260612 艦長紀要
永豐金證券 Institutional Business Group | Equity Sales
導讀摘要：本報告專為投資經理人與分析師編製，核心聚焦於 ComputeX  2026  會後供應鏈最新動態。內容
涵蓋全球半導體及 AI 伺服器庫存體檢、大模型（LLM）廠商的 Token 經濟學營收爆發期，以及共同光學封裝
（CPO/XO）在下世代架構（如台積電 NVL76）中的關鍵導入進程。報告結合最新產業調研與大會核心技術
展示（如大立光 Fiber Array），提供具備高度可操作性的產業投資視野。
一、 總體基本面與供應鏈庫存體檢
當前全球半導體及 AI 產業基本面持續上修，市場整體的強勁動能並未改變。根據大會最新釋出的調研數據，主
要環節體檢如下：
產業營收動能： 預估 2026 全年營收將達到 60% 的月增長（MoM），外圍市場與主要板塊對 2027 年的初
步預估亦高達 45% 以上。2 月份市場的劇烈修正已被證實僅為良性的漲多回檔，產業健康度無虞。

上游端庫存水位： 晶圓代工端庫存目前處於相對正常的健康水平，並無過剩風險，整體生產天數皆控制在
合理區間內。
下游與網通端庫存： 下游伺服器大廠（如 Supermicro、Dell、HPE）、ODM/OEM 廠（如鴻海、廣達、仁
寶）以及主要網通大廠（如 Cisco、Arista、Celestica）的庫存則處於相對高點，目前正處於實質去化階
段，天數雖略高但仍在控制範圍。
消費性電子與其他： PC 的 DDR5 庫存近期出現明顯的被動拉貨與備貨潮，主要受到下半年季節性需求帶
動；車用測試及代理商端庫存則普遍維持在低檔。
評價面評價（Valuation）： 由於股價於 4、5 月份飆升，費城半導體指數（SOX）的 PE 重新回到約 30 倍
的歷史高位，PB 同步創下新高，整體評價面偏高，需密切追蹤後續獲利實質兌現的速度。
二、 AI 市場需求與 Token 經濟學模型
大模型廠（LLM）的算力消耗與 Token  使用量正在重塑整個軟硬體產業鏈的利潤分配，Token  經濟學
（Tokenomics）已迎來實質營收爆發期。

### Page 2

1. 算力與 Token 消耗量反彈
今年以來 Token  總使用量呈現階梯式走高。雖然 4  月份因主要模型廠調整定價、部分廠商算力不足（如
Anthropic 算力出現擠兌，限制客戶使用天數）導致短期用量下滑，但隨著基礎建設補強（如 Anthropic 與 xAI
敲定大型資料中心協議），近期算力瓶頸已順利打開，用量大幅反彈。
2. 大模型廠營收（ARR）幾何級數增長
Anthropic 與 OpenAI 兩家頂級模型廠，今年度合計營收預估將直接跨過 1,500 億美元 大關。Anthropic 目前的
ARR 已由去年 1 月的 1 至 2 億美元飆升至現階段的 400 至 500 億美元，一年半內成長達 40 倍，年底 ARR 目
標設定在 1,000 至 1,200 億美元之間。
3. 差異化定價與資本支出上修
模型廠開始採取極為嚴格的「差別定價」。針對免費或低價值 Token 容忍更長的延遲時間；而願意付費的企業
客戶，則提供每百萬 Token 高達 40 至 50 美元的高階高流量服務。此舉讓模型廠在 Token 消耗總量可控的前

提下，營收表現大幅優於預期。在現金流快速回籠的刺激下，預計未來數月內，各大 CSP 廠將再度上修 2027
年的資本支出（CapEx）預算（目前 2026  年預估值已達 7,000  億美元，2027  年市場共識普遍在 8,000  至
9,000 億美元之間）。
永豐金證券 Institutional Business Group | 內部研究資料 2

### Page 3

三、 共同光學封裝（CPO/XO）關鍵進程與台積電下世代架構
傳統銅線傳輸在高頻下已達物理極限。當速度推升至 448G Series 以上時，訊號損耗極快，傳統 PCB 板上的
傳輸距離極限被壓縮至僅剩 5 公分，推動下世代架構全面倒向光通訊。
1. 台積電與 Ayar Labs 的深層綁定
在共同光學封裝領域中，由 Intel、NVIDIA、台積電共同投資的新創公司 Ayar  Labs（星路科技）扮演核心角
色。台積電在投資 Ayar  Labs  後，已將其技術與自身的先進製程及封裝平台深度整合（原本 Ayar  Labs  仰賴
GlobalFoundries 的晶圓代工，現已全面轉向台積電製程）。
2. 外部光引擎（XO）新規格躍居主流
面對傳統 400G/800G 光模組的瓶頸，市場現階段正全力探討 **XO（外部光引擎）** 的新規格。XO 本身的尺
寸約為傳統光模組的兩倍，內部不再放置 DSP，而是透過高密度的 64 條通道（單通道速度 200G）一舉將頻
寬推升至 12.8T。此架構能顯著節省功耗並改善過熱問題。

3. 台積電先進架構：跨機櫃 NVL76 技術平台
台積電在本次技術大會上，首度揭露了代號 **NVL76** 的新一代先進架構設計。這並非傳統單一機櫃（Rack）
的內部 Scale-Up，而是透過 CPO  技術，將多達 8  個機櫃進行跨機櫃的 NVL  高速訊號互聯（Cross-Rack
Scale-Up）。
機櫃配置： 採用 9 + 18 + 9 的物理配置（9 個 Compute Tray、18 個 Switch Tray、9 個 Compute Tray），
其中 Switch 內部全面搭載 4 顆 Quantum-5 高速交換晶片。
頻寬配置： 根據最新架構推算，未來的先進架構中，單一 GPU 將直接對應 6 個 3.2T 的外部光模組，對光
通訊零組件的需求量較前代呈現高達 6 倍的爆發式增長。
4. 先進光學封裝供應鏈核心解密
封裝階段 技術核心 主要供應商與合作方案

晶片）整合測試 愛德萬（Advantest）測試機台 + FormFactor 探針卡。

Offset）則由訊芯（Susemihl）切入。

### Page 4

5. 大立光（Largan）Fiber Array 技術重大進展
大立光在本次大會展示的 **Fiber Array（光纖陣列）** 出現技術性突破。過往產品多為 1 排 40 條光纖，大立
光首度成功交付多達 4 排、高達 160 條的高密度光纖陣列。此技術最困難之處在於玻璃 V-Groove（V型槽）的
超精密切割，半導體對位端公差要求僅 0.3  微米（µm）。大立光克服了多排組裝下的公差累積效應，成為
1.6T 及 3.2T 世代下，台積電先進封裝體系中不可或缺的光學戰略合作夥伴。
四、 晶圓代工先進製程與主要處理器競爭局勢
台積電 SoIC (3D 堆疊) 技術路徑： 隨晶片物理尺寸逼近 Reticle 限制，良率成本大幅拉高。台積電的先進封
裝策略將全面由傳統 2.5D（CoWoS）移向 **SoIC（3D 堆疊）**。包括 HBM 記憶體控制層及核心 IO 元
件，在未來的晶片設計中都將優先採用 3D 堆疊方式與運算核心進行垂直整合。
NVIDIA Vera 處理器的市場制約： NVIDIA 新一代處理器 **Vera** 展現出極強的通路支配力。其核心策略在

於「買處理器直接配套/綁定 HBM 產能」，在目前全球先進封裝與高階記憶體極度短缺的市況下，此一綑綁
策略精準解決了客戶的產能痛點，預計年底前將為 NVIDIA 注入龐大營收。
Google TPU v6 (Octopus) 產能指標： Google 自研的下世代處理器 Octopus（TPU v6）是明年（2027）
市場上唯一採用超大尺寸（達 8 至 9 個 Reticle 以上）的超巨型先進封裝晶片，內部整合 4 個運算 die、4
個 IO 控制 die 及 8 顆高階 HBM。該晶片的製程良率與出貨進度，將直接成為全球先進代工製程產能與良率
的最高指標。
報告聲明：本文件為 Institutional Business Group 內部研究資料，僅供專業投資經理人及分析師參考，請勿對外傳閱。

### Page 2

FIFA2026世足賽經濟效益商機可期
1資料來源：Statista、FIFA 、中信投顧整理
世足賽
百年來世界盃主辦國
• FIFA 世界盃(World Cup)由國際足球總會(FIFA)主辦，每 4 年舉辦一次，是全球規模最大、收
視率最高、影響力最深遠的單項體育賽事，其狂熱程度與吸金能力甚至超越奧運會。
• FIFA2026的參賽隊伍擴增至48隊(+50%)，總場次達104場(+62%)，將實質帶動球衣、足球鞋
及周邊裝備消耗量增長 。
• 上屆世足創造約170億美元經濟效益，本屆結合美洲主場強大消費力，相關品牌商機與營收規
模有望創歷史新高。而台股製鞋與紡織代工廠隨世足賽效益，帶動需求和拉貨力道提升。
歷年FIFA收入趨勢 (USD,B)

### Page 3

世足賽帶動旅遊、廣播、運動用品及相關消費需求
2資料來源： FIFA，中信投顧整理
世足賽
世足賽受惠行業經濟效益
• FIFA2026舉辦時間為2026/6/11/~7/19，由美加墨三國聯合舉辦，主辦城市高達16座，可望
帶動旅遊、飯店、房產、廣播、運動用品等消費經濟產業收入動能。
受惠產業 / 領域 經濟效益說明（核心亮點）
廣告和贊助 廣告商和品牌可以通過贊助世界盃賽事來提升品牌曝光度，吸引全球觀眾的注意。
體育賭博 世界盃期間體育賭博活動會大幅增加，這為賭博公司和相關機構帶來了龐大的收入。
旅遊業 世界盃吸引了大量球迷前往參加比賽，可為當地的旅遊業帶來了巨大的商機，包括住宿、餐飲、交通等。
電子商務 球迷會在賽事期間增加購買球衣、紀念品等商品，這為電子商務平台和零售商帶來了商機。
媒體和網絡平台 世界盃賽事的轉播權和網絡直播權是非常有價值的資產，媒體和網路平台可以通過播放賽事來吸引廣告收
入。
賽事觀眾收入 大量的觀眾會購買門票，這對主辦國的經濟有直接的影響。
基建城市發展 世界盃的舉辦通常需要大規模的基礎設施建設，這對城市的發展有積極的影響。

2026年世足賽贊助商
3資料來源： FIFA、各公司官網，中信投顧整理
世足賽
2026年FIFA主要合作夥伴及贊助商
• 2026年世足賽的贊助商體系主要分為三個級別：FIFA官方合作夥伴、世界盃官方贊助商以及賽
事支持商與供應商。麥當勞、樂事、百威英博等全球餐飲與零售龍頭依舊可見。
• 現代贊助不再只是在球場圍欄看板刷存在感，更強調數位變現與會員忠誠度計畫，各大贊助商
紛紛透過官方 APP 推出賽事預測、限定虛擬與實體周邊，甚至開放會員以點數兌換極其珍貴
的世足賽門票或頂級觀賽體驗，藉此深度綁定消費者。
第一級：FIFA 合作夥伴 第二級：世界盃官方贊助商 第三級：區域官方支持者

有國際足聯旗下所有賽事
與推廣項目的獨家權益。
本屆世界盃專屬全球合作夥伴，圍
繞美加墨世界盃大賽展開全方位的
商業推廣與曝光。

量，並透過與客戶合作的專屬行銷活動帶動加值服務收益。

股價相對大盤走勢

聯電 (2303 TT)  Buy

新產品帶動中長期成長，調升至 Buy，目標價至 168 元。  
投資建議 ：展望 2026 年，聯電認為 AI 相關領域仍將是 2026 年
半導體產業的主要成長驅動力。此外，隨著邊緣  AI 應用的持
續商業化部署，用於通用型伺服器的晶片需求預計也將復甦。
相比之下，記憶體供需失衡的負面影響可能會對部分消費性電
子產品帶來壓力。但整體而言，半導體產業預計在  2026 年將
YoY+14~16% 。晶圓代工市場預計成長約 YoY+20% ，預估聯電
成長將會高於所在市場的平均成長。預估聯電 2026 年稅後 EPS 
5.59 元。公司在先進封裝、矽光子及 Intel 合作的 12nm 陸續取
得進展，為  2027 年的中長期成長奠定基礎，有利業績向上。考
量聯電的  ASP 正處於從低谷回升並結構性轉強的階段，近期
市場對成熟製程晶圓代工產業評價上修，反映非中系成熟製程
之戰略價值提升 ， 因此調升至Buy， 目標價168 元 (2026PER30X) 。 
1Q26 稅後 EPS 1.29 元：聯電 1Q26 營收為 610.38 億元， QoQ-
1.25% 。聯電  1Q26 整體出貨量約當  12 吋晶圓  102.1 萬片，
QoQ+2.7% 。1Q26 產能利用率小幅增加至  79% 。ASP 因年初
價格調整而下滑。 1Q26 產能利用率微增至 79%，ASP QoQ-1% 。
1Q26 毛利率下滑 1.48 個百分點至 29.19% 。業外認列 53.67 億
元收益，來自利息收益 0.88 億元，匯兌收益 3.03 億元。認列投
資收益 49.97 億元 (約貢獻 30% 獲利，大都為手中持有的有價證
券評價，並未處份，後續視股市場波動 )，聯電 1Q26 稅後 EPS 
1.29 元。  
預估 2Q26 稅後 EPS 1.41 元：聯電預期通訊領域在 2Q26 將反
彈，消費性電子由  MCU 、 LCD 控制  IC 及電源相關產品等延
續成長力道。 預期 2H26 表現會優於 1H26， 主要是因為 HV（高
壓）、NVM（非揮發性記憶體） 、PMIC（電源管理  IC ）和 RFSOI 
等特殊製程專案將在 2H26 量產。預估聯電 2Q26 晶圓出貨量
QoQ+7~9% ， 2Q26 聯電產能利用率約 81~83% 。 ASP in USD 
QoQ+1~3% ，毛利率微增至 30% 左右。預估聯電 2Q26 稅後 EPS 
1.41 元。 1Q26 稅率偏低約 3%， 2Q26 用平均 15% 預估。

2 群益投顧 
  聯電 ESG ：  
聯電在 ESG 方面持續展現積極作為，並獲國內外多項評鑑肯定：  
永續策略與治理： ESG 由董事會與二位共同總經理組成的指導委員會全盤
規劃，持續以 「以人為本、與環境共生、與社會共榮」 為願景推動永續。 ESG
績效結合員工分紅與限制型股票條件，提升全員參與動能與企業凝聚力。  
環境面（ E）  
2025 年獲國際永續評鑑（如 FTSE Russell ）評為全台 ESG 表現頂尖企業，
半導體業唯一入選前 3。  
持續推動用水回收、能源效率提升，製程用水回收率在各廠均高於法規標
準。  
積極採購並投資離岸風電，與 CIP 渢妙風場簽 30 年綠電採購合約，展現對
2050 淨零承諾。  
社會面（ S）  
深化產學合作，與台灣 4 所大學半導體專班合作， 600 多位學生參與。  
啟動員工持股信託，落實多元薪酬政策，提升員工福祉並吸引人才。  
長期投入社會公益，如偏鄉關懷、青年培力、綠色創新等持續進行。  
公司治理（ G）  
董事會組成高度多元且獨立，獨董占三分之二、女性董事占三分之一。  
強化利害關係人溝通與資訊透明度，持續強化供應鏈 ESG 要求，並納入評
核與管理系統，推動價值鏈共榮。

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌，亦將出具降評報告。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

南亞科 (2408 TT)  Trading Buy

系統性風險，調降至 Trading Buy 。  
投資建議 ： Kioxia(258A JP) 、 SanDisk(SNDK US) 、 Solidigm 及
Cisco(CSCO US) 認購南亞科私募普通股，對南亞科而言，此為
具策略意義的合作，除挹注資金外，亦有助於綁定長期客戶需
求，並提升拓展未來 CSP 等大客戶的機會。供給端方面， DRAM
大廠持續將資本支出與產能轉向 HBM 與先進製程 DDR5，壓縮
傳統 DDR4 與 LPDDR4 供給；需求端受惠 AI 伺服器與邊緣運
算帶動記憶體搭載量提升，支撐整體 DRAM 報價維持高檔。在
價格上行帶動下，預估南亞科 2026 年稅後 EPS 達 54.47 元。過
去 3 個月記憶體產業面臨多項利空雜音，包括現貨價格波動、
中國供給擴張疑慮、終端需求放緩，及近期市場對 NVIDIA Vera
平台 SOCAMM2 配置調整的擔憂。然而， DRAM 及 NAND 產品
報價持續上漲，顯示產業供需結構及基本面未出現明顯轉弱跡
象。考量短期市場波動加劇，因此將投資評等調降至 Trading 
Buy ，待市場情緒及籌碼結構趨穩後再重新檢視投資機會。  
1Q26 稅後 EPS 7.55 元：1Q26 營收為 490.87 億元 ，QoQ+63.11% ，
南亞科 DRAM 平均銷售價格較 4Q25 增加 ，ASP 約 QoQ+>70% 。
匯率對營收影響 QoQ+1~+3% ，Shipment QoQ-4~-6% 。毛利率受
Blend ASP 上升與匯率有利影響 ， 1Q26 毛利率從 4Q25 的 49.04%
上揚至 67.87% ，增加 18.83 個百分點。 1Q26 稅後 EPS 7.55 元，
淨值 62.25 元。  
2026 年資本支出提升至 520 億元 ：南亞科董事會通過 2026 年
的資本支出預算以不超過新台幣 520 億元為上限， YoY+287% ，
其中 62 億元是 2025 年遞延至 2026 年，資本支出中 70% 用在新
廠的廠務無塵室等，剩下 30% 則會使用在設備採購上。預估新
廠將在 2027 年初裝機， 1H27 正式量產，規劃是 1H28 可以達到
月產能 15~20K 片 /月的目標，以 DDR5 為主。目前 DDR5 占南
亞科約 10% ，公司目標是維持在 10% 以上並逐步提升，但仍需
視整體缺貨結構而定。

2 群益投顧 
  南亞科 ESG ：  
佈局綠色科技生產  
DRAM 為智慧世代的關鍵零組件，南亞科研發先進且具高效能的 「環境友善
產品」以符合市場與客戶需求。所有產品於設計初期時，即導入生命週期思
維與綠色設計，透過綠色產品推動委員會進行綠色產品管理。

重視人才發展與培養  
「人才」是公司創新、營運與永續發展的根本，強化公司全方位人才培育與
發展，南亞科技與長庚大學簽署成立記憶體學程碩士班，學生除可獲高額獎
學金，更可獲得畢業後的就業機會。南亞科技期盼從育才、用才、引才、聚
才、留才各方面，強化臺灣記憶體產業在國際上的競爭力。

共同攜手永續夥伴  
南亞科攜手上下游合作夥伴一同前行， 295 家供應商認同公司的永續理念，
一同簽署南亞科技企業社會責任承諾書且簽署率達 100% 、輔導供應商進行
永續改善，稽核、輔導與改善率均 100% 完成，並持續辦理供應鏈研討會。
此外，也與供應商共同合作推動人權及環境相關的議題。

永續績效備受肯定  
南亞科視 ESG 為企業的重要承諾，南亞科追求經營績效的同時，也同步落
實 ESG 的平衡發展。南亞科技參加 2025 第 18 屆台灣企業永續獎評選，公
司已連續第九年獲頒多項殊榮，獲獎包括年度最佳報告 (製造業 )、台灣 100
大永續典範企業獎及四項永續單項績效  – 氣候領袖獎、創新成長領袖獎、
資訊安全領袖獎與透明誠信領袖獎。

預期近期股價將處於盤整

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌，亦將出具降評報告。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

2458 義隆(ELAN)

報告日期： 2026/06/11 研 究 員：羅  金 鳳 (Sunny Lo)  
壹、投 資 建 議

一. 2Q26 記憶體及 CPU 缺貨影響營運表現  
1Q26 因記憶體及 CPU 缺貨漲價 ， 部份客戶提前下單 ， 加上Touch 
Screen 轉 HAPTIC 單價提高以及 Fingerprint 出貨成長，帶動義
隆電 1Q26 營收 32.25 億元， QoQ+12.76% ，YoY+3.39% ，但受
晶圓代工及封測成本上升及高毛利率產品營收比重下降影響，毛
利率由 4Q25 的 49.29% 下滑至 47.65% ，稅後淨利 7.05 億元，  
QoQ-4.15% ，YoY+28.56% ，稅後 EPS 2.32 元，符合預期。

2Q26 仍受記憶體及 CPU 缺貨影響，打亂客戶下單節奏，不過
Haptic 及 Fingerprint 的需求仍相對較好 ， 使2Q26 營運持續成長 ，
惟因全球 NB 可能呈現年減 10～15% ，義隆電雖然每台 NB 的內
含價值提升，但對 2H26 看法仍相對謹慎， 2H26 可能僅比 1H26
小幅成長，綜上，我們預估義隆電 2Q26 營收 33.02 億元，
QoQ+2.39% ，YoY+8.91% ，晶圓代工及封測成本上升仍壓抑毛利
率表現，預估為 48.01% ，稅後淨利 6.67 億元，  QoQ-5.39% ，
YoY+88.55% ，稅後 EPS 2.2 元。

二. 2026 年 HAPTIC 、無人機、 ADAS 營收逐漸增加  
義隆電持續進產品多元化， 2026 年包括 NB 及 Non-NB 的產品將
有明顯進展，其中應用於 NB 的 HAPTIC 於 2022 年即開始出貨，
但過去 3～4 年為耕耘期，出貨量的成長相對較慢，由於 2026 年
起 Windows 的作業軟體 (OS) 開始內嵌 HAPTIC 的功能 ， 同時美系
客戶將其全系列的 NB 導入 HAPTIC 功能，義隆電也將成本由 20
美元降至 10 美 元，有 助HAPTIC 加速導入 。 由於客戶帶有HPTIC
功能的新機種於 4～5/2026 上市， 1Q26 已開始進行 HAPTIC 模
組的鋪貨，帶動義隆電 1Q26 出貨量就大於 2025 年全年的出貨
量，預估 2H26 的鋪貨將持續成長，為 2H26 營運重要動能。

無人機及 ADAS 是義隆電近年在 NB 之外積極耕耘的項目，其中
無人機方面，義隆電在 2025 年的出貨型態為晶片， IPS 晶片於
3Q25 開始出貨 ， 由於義隆電的雲台及避障功能模組可比競爭對手
有低功耗 (可省去散熱板 )，模組體積也較輕薄短小的優點，成為客
戶 Cost down 最佳選擇，且義隆電 Mini-LED 的 Local dimming
面板在極寒的氣候、戴手套的情況下也可操作靈敏，且具防水、
觸控功能，因此，在國內已與數家無人機標案公司合作，模組化
投資評等：買進

的高 ASP 將帶動義隆電 2H26 來自無人機的營收貢獻明顯放大。
在 ADAS 方面，義隆電的 IC 可抓取人臉進行辨識，也可做盲點偵
測系統，目前已應用於電動巴士及物流車，下一步將導入計程車，

三. 預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 10.34 元 
2026 年在 AI PC 滲透率上升、商用機種出貨比重上升、 HAPTIC
貢獻大幅成長、無人機出貨以高單價的模組化為主，帶動義隆電
2026 年營運大幅優於 NB 產業的表現 ， 因此 ， 我們預估義隆電2026
年營收 141.3 億元 ，YoY+14.63%，毛 利 率47.92%，稅 後 淨 利31.43
億元， YoY+28.7% ，稅後 EPS 10.34 元。

四. 投資建議為買進，目標價 176 元 
雖然目前因全球經濟環境不確定性高，但義隆電持續進行技術升
級及產品組合的優化，獲利表現仍能較全球 NB 產業的狀況佳，
邊緣 AI 的發展加速將帶動 2026 年 AI PC 的滲透率優於 2025 年，
有利義隆電的 Haptic 及 Fingerprint 出貨量成長 ， 加上義隆電晶片
設計的附加價值將比過往明顯提升，反映在 ASP 上將比傳統機種
高出 10% 以上，有利營運在大環境不佳的情況下保持韌性，加上
成功脫胎換骨，打入軍用無人機及進軍矽光子領域，有利未來營
運表現，因此，投資建議維持買進，目標價 176 元(17x2026 稅後
EPS) 。

貳、公 司 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介  
義隆電 (2458) 成立 1994 年 ， 為全球知名的人機介面晶片領導廠商
之一，專精於觸控螢幕晶片 (Touchscreen Controller) 、帶筆功能
的觸控螢幕晶片 (Touchscreen Controller with Pen) 、觸控板模組  
(Touchpad Module) 、 指向裝置(Pointing Stick) 及生物辨識晶片 (含
指紋與人臉辨識 )的研發及整體解決方案，主要應用在智慧手機、
平板、筆記型電腦以及各式消費性電子產品，其中，在全球筆電
應用之晶片市場  ( 觸控螢幕晶片、觸控板模組及指向裝置在全球
筆電市場的市占率位居第一  ) 居於領導地位。由於電子科技進步
快速，資訊安全問題為近年業界關注的重點，義隆未來將朝資訊

二.營運概況及展望

1、預估 2026 年全球 NB 出貨 YoY-5.26% 
在新冠疫情過後，及通膨的影響，品牌廠持續進行庫存去化，使
全球 NB 需求在 2022 年及 2023 年急速下降，在進行約 6～8 個
季度的庫存去化後，需求開始回穩，雖然要回到疫情期間的年增
雙位數不易 ， 但需求大致走穩 ， 使2024 年全球出貨量回升到 1.95
億台，YoY+2.42% ，2025 年在 Windows 10 EOL 及疫情期間購買
的 NB 逐漸進入換機潮的帶動下 ， 全球NB 出貨量持續呈現小幅成
長，預估為 2.02 億台， YoY+3.13% ，預估 2026 年在 AI 訓練需求
強勁了 3 年後 ， 推論的需求將開始接棒成長 ， 有利AI PC 的導入，
帶動全球 NB 成長，不過因為 AI 強勁的需求，且進入推理的代理
人 AI 後，對這憶體需求大幅成長，帶動 2Q25 後記憶體需求大幅
成長，呈現明顯供不應求，帶動記憶體報價爆漲，在記憶體價格
漲勢未歇的情況下，將影響終端的消費性電子產品，因此，預估
2026 年全球 NB 出貨量將下滑至 1.71 億台， YoY-15.18% 。

義隆(2458) 
買進, TP 210 (潛在報酬 34.19%)  
買進 
Price(06/08):156.5 
Target Price：210

上半年客戶的急單讓營收呈現淡季不淡，同時 AI 影像辨識產品的
貢獻也逐步發酵，26Q2 已來到雙位數水準，預計隨著無人機產品
從單晶片轉往整體模組方案 ， 倍數成長的單價將進一步帶動營收的
增長。此外義隆也透過投資 PIC 設計公司 PETA 來切入矽光子領
域，期望共同開發出下一代 3.2T EIC 產品。綜合以上，研究部預
估義隆 2026 年 EPS 9.50 元，目前 PE 為 16.4x，參考歷史 PE 區
間為 12-24x，考量公司開始往無人機、矽光子領域布局，前者將
開始貢獻營收，後者則會逐步發表新品，讓義隆擺脫過往受 NB 景
氣波動的影響，因此給予 22xPER，目標價 210 元，建議買進。 
⚫ 公司基本資料 ⚫ 公司基本資料 
義隆(2458)成立於 1994 年，為國內晶圓設計廠，產品應用聚焦於
NB 且多為全球領域龍頭 ， 觸控板及觸控螢幕晶片及Point Stick 市
佔率高達 50%以上，2025 年產品組合包含觸控板 46%、觸控螢幕
晶片 22%、 指紋辨識12%、Point Stick 13%、MCU 2%及其他 3%。
銷售地區則為香港 73%、中國 18%、台灣 7%、其他 2%，競爭對
手主要為 Synaptics。

第一金證券投資顧問股份有限公司(First Capital Management Inc.) 台北市長安東路一段 35 號 3F   TEL(02) 2563 -1666

日 週 月 ⚫ 近況更新 
客戶急單挹注，26Q1 淡季不淡 
義隆 26Q1 營收 32.2 億元 ，Q+12.8%，Y+3.4%， 毛利率47.63%，
稅後淨利 7.1 億元，Q-4.2%，Y+28.6%，EPS 2.46 元。零組件的
短缺和記憶體成本的上漲，讓客戶加急下單， 營收表現呈現淡季不
淡。然而產品組合的變化也讓整體毛利率略為下滑，費用則有部分
政府的補助讓整體本業獲利較上季度成長。

AI 產品貢獻逾雙位數，產品組合有望轉佳 
義隆 5 月營收 10.0 億元，M-12.0%，Y+3.6%，4 月營收 11.4 億
元，M-6.7%，Y+6.3%，4-5 月合計 21.5 億元，Y+5.0%。客戶端
持續增加零組件的庫存備貨，同時 AI 影像辨識產品的營收貢獻也
逐步拉高，4-5 月合計貢獻營收 11%，因此公司展望 26Q2 營收維
持 32-34 億元水準 ， 毛利率則預期產品組合相對改善下 ， 預估區間
為 46.5-49.5%，然而光罩費用及展覽等支出也讓整體費用水準提
升，因此預估 OPM 區間為 21.4-25.9%。綜合以上，研究部預估
義隆 26Q2 營收 33.4 億元 ，Q+3.5%，Y+10.1%， 毛利率48.13%，
稅後淨利 6.7 億元，Q-4.6%，Y+90.1%，EPS 2.21 元。

無人機產品發酵，切入矽光子領域 
自 25Q3 起義隆 AI 影像辨識產品已開始正式量產出貨，營收貢獻
也在上半年提升至雙位數占比，除了車用 ADAS 產品外，無人機
的 ISP 晶片貢獻也將跟隨與無人機廠雷虎及其他合作夥伴出貨增
加， 此外義隆產品也將從單晶片轉往 Camera 模組甚至整個通訊模
組，產品單價將為數倍成長，年底更有望開始往三光鏡頭酬載產品
發展 ， 產品單價又將會再往另一水準躍升 ， 另外地面飛控站( GCS )
也會從面板模組到含指紋辨識、陣列天線等整組方案出貨，期望在
非紅供應鏈趨勢下，全球年百萬架的無人機需求帶動出貨的增長。
-1.26 -6.29 3.3

3 
另外義隆也將切往矽光領域，透過投資 PETA Optroni cs，義隆將
開始研發下一代 3.2T EIC 產品，目前PETA Optronics 已有 800G
及 1.6T PIC 產品，因此研究部正向看待公司在未來透過與 PIC 設
計公司的合作共同推出 OE 產品來切入矽光子領域 。 至於在NB 產
品方面，由於上半年客戶的急單拉貨，使營收表現呈現淡季不淡，
然而公司同時也時刻觀察客戶庫存水準來調整出貨節奏 ， 以免重回
先前 Overbooking 的情況，另外由於上游代工和封測廠成本的上
漲，公司預計將於 7 月開始漲價，同樣有助於產品獲利率的提升。
綜合以上，研究部預估義隆 2026 年營收 135.4 億元，Y+9.8%，
毛利率 48.02%，稅後淨利 28.9 億元，Y+18.2%，EPS 9.50 元。

結論 
上半年客戶的急單讓營收呈現淡季不淡，同時 AI 影像辨識產品的
貢獻也逐步發酵，26Q2 已來到雙位數水準，預計隨著無人機產品
從單晶片轉往整體模組方案 ， 倍數成長的單價將進一步帶動營收的
增長。此外義隆也透過投資 PIC 設計公司 PETA 來切入矽光子領
域，期望共同開發出下一代 3.2T EIC 產品。綜合以上，研究部預
估義隆 2026 年 EPS 9.50 元，目前 PE 為 16.4x，參考歷史 PE 區
間為 12-24x，考量公司開始往無人機、矽光子領域布局，前者將
開始貢獻營收，後者則會逐步發表新品，讓義隆擺脫過往受NB 景
氣波動的影響，因此給予 22xPER，目標價 210 元，建議買進。

Goldman Sachs (Asia) L.L.C.
Hon Hai (2317.TW): May rev +40% YoY; AI servers remains key growth
driver; Buy (on CL)
5 June 2026 | 7:29PM HKT
Hon Hai’s May revenues were +40% YoY/+3% MoM to NT$859bn, or 5% higher
than our previous estimates. From a MoM perspective, Consumer electronics
increased MoM on strong pull-in momentum, followed by Cloud & Networking (flat
MoM), then PC segment (slightly decreased MoM post pull-in) and Component &
Others (down MoM on high base in Apr).  From a YoY perspective, Component &

Others delivered YoY growth on shipment increase, Cloud & Networking sustained
strong YoY growth, driven by the AI cloud products, PC revenues performed
increased YoY on new products growth momentum; Consumer electronics saw
significant gr owth YoY due to order pull-in momentum. Management expects the
2Q26 performance is tracking well above company’s previously anticipated
significant gr owth, while the company continues to monitor the global political and

economic situation. Although 2Q is a traditionally slow season of production
transition, management expect revenues from AI racks to sustain growth.
We expect continued growth of the AI server business with market share gain
opportunities, along with the upcoming smartphone form factor changes in 2026E to
drive Hon Hai’s growth ahead. Maintain Buy (on CL).
3-month revenues preview: We expect 2Q26E revenues to be +31% YoY/+11%
QoQ to NT$2.4 trillion. May continues MoM growth (+3%) off Apr’s high base,

reflecting the solid demand of AI server racks despite the slow seasonality of
traditional electronics products in 2Q.  We expect Jun revenues to grow 22% YoY (or
-23% MoM) considering the high base, which is in line with the historical seasonality.

GS Factor Profile
The Goldman Sachs Factor Profile provides investment context for a stock by comparing key attributes to the market (i.e. our universe of rated stocks)
and its sector peers. The four key attributes depicted are: Growth, Financial Returns, Multiple (e.g. valuation) and Integrated (a c omposite of Growth,
Financial Returns and Multiple). Growth, Financial Returns and Multiple are calculated by using normalized ranks for specific metrics f or each stock. The

normalized ranks for the metrics are then averaged and converted into percentiles for the relevant attribute. The precise calcula tion of each metric may
vary depending on the fiscal y ear, industry and region, but the standard approach is as follows:
Growth is based on a stock’s forward-looking sales growth, EBITDA growth and EPS growth (for financial s tocks, only EPS and sales growth), with a
higher percentile indicating a higher growth company. Financial Returns is based on a stock’s forward-looking ROE, ROCE and CROCI (for financial

stocks, only ROE), with a higher percentile indicating a company with higher financial r eturns. Multiple is based on a stock’s forward-looking P/E, P/B,
price/dividend (P/D), EV/EBITDA, EV/FCF and EV/Debt Adjusted Cash Flow (DACF) (for financial s tocks, only P/E, P/B and P/D), with a higher percentile
indicating a stock trading at a higher multiple. The Integrated percentile is calculated as the average of the Growth percentile, Financial Returns

percentile and (100% - Multiple percentile).
Financial Returns and Multiple use the Goldman Sachs analyst forecasts at the fiscal y ear-end at least three quarters in the future. Growth uses inputs
for the fiscal y ear at least seven quarters in the future compared with the year at least three quarters in the future (on a per-share basis for all metrics).
For a more detailed description of how we calculate the GS Factor Profile, please c ontact your GS representative.

M&A Rank
Across our global coverage, we examine stocks using an M&A framework, considering both qualitative factors and quantitative factors (which may vary
across sectors and regions) to incorporate the potential that certain companies could be acquired. We then assign a M&A rank as a means of sc oring
companies under our rated coverage from 1 to 3, with 1 representing high (30%-50%) probability of the company becoming an acquisition targ et, 2
representing medium (15%-30%) probability and 3 representing low (0%-15%) probability. For companies ranked 1 or 2, in line with our s tandard

departmental guidelines we incorporate an M&A component into our target price. M&A rank of 3 is considered immaterial and theref ore does not
factor into our price target, and may or may not be discussed in research.
Quantum
Quantum is Goldman Sachs’ proprietary database providing access to detailed financial statement histories, forecasts and ratios. It can be used for
in-depth analysis of a single company, or to make comparisons between companies in differ ent sectors and markets.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a B uy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not C overed), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region ’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the r eturn across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment R eview Committee or

other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such s tocks.
Total return potential represents the upside or downside differ ential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all cov ered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership .

### Page 1

ab 5 June 2026
Global Research
Chipbond Technology
Rising demand and content in optical business
New opportunities for optical transceiver business
We see upside in Chipbond's SiPh business on stronger demand for 800G/1.6T optical
transceivers, new customer wins and increasing content. We now expect overall 800G
and above transceiver shipments to grow at a 99% CAGR in 2025-28 (prior forecast at
77%), reaching 235m units in 2028, while chips adopting gold bumping could see

much faster growth. Chipbond was qualified by its US fabless customer for TIA chips and
recently  obtained  qualifications from new customers for PD and modulator ICs. It
continues to work on qualification from other customers, pointing to upside in its
revenue and earnings from 2027E. Our industry checks  indicate Chipbond will start to
provide backend services (dicing and testing) from 2027E alongside gold bumping,
offering 50-100% additional value-add vs bumping only in the SiPh  business. We raise

SiPh sales contribution to 5% for 2026E (4% prior),  13% for 2027E (8% prior),  and
24% for 2028E (14% prior) on new project wins.
Stronger bumping capability to keep Chipbond ahead of peers
Chipbond was founded as a gold bumping supplier 30 years ago and has the ability to
design and manufacture key equipment internally. We believe its superior gold bumping
know-how, sizeable 8" bumping capacity (currently 60-70% utilised, by our estimate),
and the newly built capacity in Penang, Malaysia, to meet customers' non-China, non-

Taiwan (NCNT) requirements, will help it to keep its dominant position for RF and SiPh
bumping in the next several years. Moreover, it is working with its major shareholder
UMC on the next-generation  400G-per-lane high-speed solution with the new TFLN
(Thin-Film Lithium Niobate) material, which may require different UBM (under bump
metallisation). We note its peer ChipMOS is also evaluating entry into the SiPh market,
but we believe it will take at least 6-12 months for it to catch up , achieve qualification

and start mass production, assuming it has similar bumping know-how to Chipbond.
Q226 revenue tracking better
Chipbond's April sales came in at NT$2,185m, up 7% MoM and 24% YoY, mainly
driven by a higher ASP . With better OLED DDI build,  price hikes and upward SiPh order
revisions, we now expect its Q226 sales to grow 12% QoQ (our prior forecast was 5%
growth). We also forecast its GM to expand by 3.7ppt QoQ to 27.2% on better pricing,
and expect its GM to further improve in H226 as it gradually ramps SiPh output.

Valuation: Raise PE-based price target from NT$230 to NT$340; reiterate Buy
We  raise our 2026E/27E/28E  EPS by 1%/8%/21%, primarily reflecting a stronger SiPh
demand and higher content value from providing backend services. We raise our PT
from NT$230 to NT$340, based on 35x 2027-28E average PE ( prior 27x) vs  a 7-52x
historical range, supported by a 43% EPS CAGR for 2025-28E. We believe the brighter
earnings outlook from higher ASPs and SiPh opportunities has not been priced in. We

expect the stock to rise further as consensus EPS estimates are raised.

### Page 2

Chipbond Technology 5 June 2026 ab 2
Chipbond Technology UBS ResearchChipbond (6147.TW)
Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Is Chipbond's driver IC business seeing a recovery as a driver IC back-end supplier?
Yes. Chipbond saw strong demand for driver IC back-end in Q126, driven by customers' restocking
amid a higher DRAM pricing environment. Although Q226E shipment could be softer on pulled-

forward sales, the potential outsourcing by Korean customers to Taiwan's foundries/back-end could
support its shipment  from H226E. We think this has helped the company negotiate with its
customers to raise its driver IC back-end ASP by about 10% from Q226E, which should support a GM
recovery from the low-20% range in recent quarters to the high-20% to low-30% range in 2027E.
Q: Will Chipbond face mounting competition from its back-end peers in China?

Competition seems to have stabilised. China's DDI　fabless firms have been gaining share in recent
years, especially in TV/IT and LCD smartphone DDIC, and they tend to work with the local supply
chain in China.  DDIC back-end ASPs corrected in 2023-24 on intensifying competition, but  allocation
shifts have stabilised as several end-customers have requested chip production to stay outside China
due to geopolitical concerns. As a result, DDIC back-end prices have recovered in recent months.

Q: Will Chipbond diversify from its core driver IC business?
Chipbond has been diversifying from conventional DDIC  and has moved into  RFID and RF IC back-end
in the past decade. It  recently entered the SiPh market for high-speed optical transceivers by
leveraging its gold bumping technology. We expect sales of RF and SiPh for optical transceivers to
continue outperforming  DDIC, rising at a 43% sales CAGR in 2025-28E, lifting the non-driver
IC contribution to about 36% of total sales in 2028E (up from around 20% in 2024-25).

We reiterate our Buy rating and raise our EPS forecasts by 1%/8%/21% for 2026E/27E/28E,
reflecting stronger SiPh demand and higher content value. We raise our price target from NT$230.00
to NT$340.00, based on a 35x 2027-28E average PE from prior 27x (vs  7-52x historical range). We
believe a more favourable ASP environment and the optical transceiver business  ramp-up should
support an earnings  recovery after the 2025 trough. Our 35x target PE multiple is supported by a

43% EPS CAGR forecast for 2025-28E and is in line with our SOTP valuation cross-check. We are
bullish on Chipbond  due to our expectations of: 1) an EPS recovery for DDIC back-end driven by
higher ASPs and  Korean customers' outsourcing; 2) robust demand for RF IC on customers' market
share gains; and 3) new opportunities in SiPh  supporting diversification and future growth.
Chipbond's GM has contracted to a 20-23% range since Q423 on intensifying competition and

weaker demand. With Korean customers outsourcing DDIC production to Taiwan's supply chain, we
expect Chipbond's loading rate and DDIC back-end ASPs to rebound. The improving loading rate and
pricing environment are also  consistent with the views of foundries and driver IC back-end peers.
The stock is up 410% in the past three months, outperforming TAIEX's 34% gain, and is trading at
49x/33x 2026E/2027E PE, still below optical supply chain players and the average for OSAT peers. We

believe the brighter earnings outlook, driven by improving ASPs  and new opportunities in SiPh, has
not been priced in, and we expect the stock to rise further as consensus earnings estimates are raised.

NT$520.00 upside 24%/19% 44%/52% 28.6%/32.5% 19.3%/23.9%
NT$340.00 base 18%/17% 37%/49% 27.1%/30.4% 17.6%/21.6%
NT$150.00 downside 12%/13% 30%/45% 25.1%/27.2% 15.3%/18.0%
Source: UBS estimates
Risk to current share price is skewed (2.1:1) to the upside

Chipbond is trading at NT$270.00 (as of 5 June, 2026).
UPSIDE (NT$520.00): Our upside scenario factors in a brighter end-demand outlook for
consumer electronic products, higher allocation of outsourcing orders and  new project
wins for SiPh, driving faster growth in the non-driver IC business. In this scenario, sales
would grow by 24%/19% in 2026E/2027E, while GM would expand to 28.6%/32.5%,
1.5-2.1pp above our base case, resulting in an OPM of 19.3%/23.9%. Our upside

valuation of NT$520.00 is based on 45x average 2027-28E EPS.
BASE (NT$340.00.00): Our base case reflects our expectations of an improving ASP for
driver IC back-end from Q226, a ramp-up in outsourcing by Korean customers from
H226, and a robust RF and SiPh outlook.  We expect sales to grow by 18%/17% in
2026/2027E, with GM at 27.1%/30.4% versus 21.4% in 2025. This leads to OPM of
17.6%/21.6% in 2026E/2027E, up from 11.2% in 2025. Our price target of NT$340.00

is based on 35x  average 2027-28E EPS versus a historical 7-52x range.
DOWNSIDE (NT$150.00):  Our downside scenario factors in a weaker end-demand
environment, resulting in lower utilisation and slower non-driver IC growth. We assume
2026E/2027E sales growth of 12%/13% YoY with GM only improving mildly to
25.1%/27.2% on lower utilisation. This would lead to OPM of 15.3%/18.0% in
2026E/2027E, 2.3-3.6pp below our base case due to negative operating leverage. Our

downside valuation of NT$150.00 is based on 20x average 2027-28E EPS.

Forecast excess return 20.7%
Company Description
Chipbond is a semiconductor back-end supplier with 75-80% of its sales from display driver
IC (~40% from smartphone, and TV respectively, 15% from IT and 5% from auto) and 10-
15% from RF.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We value Chipbond based on a PE methodology.

Downside risks include but are not limited to: slower-than-expected RF business expansion
and weaker-than-expected tech end-market recovery.

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.
Important Information About Sustainable Investing Strategies: Sustainable investing strategies aim to consider and incorporate environmental, social and governance
(ESG) factors into investment process and portfolio construction. Strategies across geographies approach ESG analysis and incorporate the findings in a variety of ways.
Incorporating ESG factors or Sustainable Investing considerations may inhibit the portfolio manager’s ability to participate in certain investment opportunities that otherwise

would be consistent with its investment objective and other principal investment strategies. The returns on a portfolio incorporating ESG factors or Sustainable Investing
considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

Summary: Jentech reported May 2026 revenue of TWD2,313m (+0.1% MoM and +37.7% 
YoY; +20.3% YoY for 5M26), a record-high level, after market hours on 5 June 2026. Its 
QTD revenue accounted for 64.9% and 73.0%of our and Bloomberg consensus estimates 
for 2Q26 revenue, respectively.

We have a Buy (1) call, with a 12-month TP of TWD6,770, based on a PER of 73x on our 
1-year-forward EPS. For more information on the company, please refer to our latest flash 
report, Solid spec upgrade story on rising TDP trend, on 29 May 2026.

What’s the impact 
• Record-high monthly revenue for May. Jentech posted May 2026 revenue of 
TWD2,313m (+0.1% MoM and +37.7% YoY; +20.3% YoY for 5M26), setting a record 
high level. QTD revenue accounted for 64.9% and 73.0% of our (TWD7,123m, +34.3% 
QoQ and +37.5% YoY) and Bloomberg consensus estimates for 2Q26 revenue, 
respectively. Revenue mix in May is heat spreader (74.1%, +37.6% YoY), lead frame 
(9.2%, +38.5% YoY), electronic component (5.9%, +19.2% YoY), communication (0.7%, 
+56.7% YoY) and others (10.1%, +47.6% YoY).

• Rubin heat spreader spec not confirmed yet. The spec is not confirmed yet, but we

weaker-than-expected global AI server demand; slower-than-expected thermal spec 
upgrades.

Source:                         Trendforce, Morgan Stanley Research
We expect TV panel prices to start trending down from 3Q26
and IT panels from 4Q26. Moreover, emerging businesses will
take time to deliver meaningful contribution. Maintain EW on TV

panel names, with a relative preference for BOE.
TV panel prices to stay flat MoM in June: We expect mainstream 32", 43", 55", 65"
and 75" TV panel pricing to stay flat MoM for the month of June. On the demand
side, as earlier restocking since the start of the year tapers off, panel order
momentum is slowing. On the supply side, panel makers continue to demonstrate
good discipline in controlling output. We continue to believe that TV panel
shipments could be below seasonal levels, given the strong 1H26, so panel makers’

bargaining power is likely to weaken further in the coming months. Thus, our base-
case assumption remains that TV panel prices should trend down from 3Q26, but
the decline should not be significant, as supply-side discipline should provide
downside support. On a quarterly average basis, we expect TV panel prices to
increase by low single-digit % QoQ in 2Q26.
IT panel prices to stay largely stable in June: Monitor panel prices edged up 0.2%
MoM, while NBs are expected to remain flat MoM. In general, we continue to expect

IT panel prices to remain relatively stable vs. TV panels in the coming quarters.
Opportunities for glass in advanced packaging will take more time: We have been
believers in the adoption of glass in advanced packaging architectures  (
see our deep
dive report) as its larger dimensions could offer better cost efficiency. Moreover, it
can provide better electrical performance with lower signal loss, reduced mismatch
in coefficients of thermal expansion among different materials to mitigate warpage,

and greater mechanical strength to resist distortion during manufacturing and
operation. That said, the supply chain is still optimizing processes, so this remains
less of a story for 2026–27.
Risk/reward less attractive: With panel prices likely to trend down from 3Q26, we
view valuations as less attractive at this stage, especially for Taiwan names AUO
(2409.TW; 1.4x P/B) and Innolux (3481.TW; 1.9x P/B). We acknowledge potential
upside from emerging businesses, where glass could be used in advanced packaging,

but meaningful contribution will take time. In addition, panel makers are not the
only players seeking to penetrate this market. We therefore view the investment
case for these names as less compelling on this angle, given valuations already
exceed peak levels of the past few years. Within China, we prefer BOE (000725.SZ;
1.7x P/B) over TCL (000100.SZ; 1.7x P/B) due to its scale advantage and higher
concentration in the display business.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.

TV Panel Prices To See Stabilizing in June
TV panel prices started to edge up at the beginning of this year, rising 1% MoM in January,
2% MoM in February and 2% MoM in March.  April and May were largely flat,  and we see
prices stabilizing into June before order momentum potentially tapers from late 2Q26.

Panel Shipments To Be Skewed Toward 1H26

Over the past decade, TV panel shipments have averaged a 49%/51% split between 1H/2H.
In 2026, we expect the mix to skew toward 1H26, as TV brands have restocked ahead of
major promotional events, including the Winter Olympics in February, the FIFA World Cup
in June–July, and the China 618 campaign. While we see no clear evidence that these
events will significantly boost global TV demand, shipment timing has consistently shifted
between quarters around such promotions.

Display Fab Utilization to Stay at 80-85% in 2Q26
Supported by stronger TV panel shipments, display fab utilization averaged ~80% in 4Q24
and 82% in 1Q25. It declined to 78% in 2Q25 as brands turned more conservative after
earlier pull-ins tapered and the impact of China’s trade-in program faded. Utilization rose
to 80–85% in 3Q25 as restocking resumed for year-end promotions and remained at a
similar level in 4Q25 despite seasonal softness, as well as into 1Q26. We think industry

utilization can remain roughly flat QoQ at 80–85% in 2Q26, as output discipline remains
in place.
In the medium to long term, we maintain our view that the display segment is undergoing
structural change. Chinese panel makers have shifted strategically from top-line growth to
profitability, with fewer government subsidies reinforcing supply discipline. We think more
investors now believe the industry’s “production to order” model can remain sustainable,

especially as panel price cycles shortened to quarters in 2023–24 vs. multi-year cycles in
the prior decade. Consolidation moves – including LG Display’s sale of its Gen 8.5
Guangzhou fab to TCL and Sharp’s shutdown of its Gen 10 Sakai fab – also support a
healthier supply backdrop (for more information, see Display – Structural Changes on
Supply Side Not Fairly Priced In,  June 17, 2021).

Morgan Stanley Research 5
Risk/Reward Remains Less Attractive
We believe this round of panel price hikes is coming to an end, and thus risk/reward is less
attractive, especially for Taiwanese names. Below are our current views:
• AUO (2409.TW, Derrick Yang): We are EW on AUO,  with a PT of NT$14.00, based
on a  0.7x 2026e P/B multiple. Although TV panel price hikes are ongoing, we think
the valuation of 1.4x 2026e P/B has  priced in all favorable dynamics vs. its mid-

cycle average of 0.8x since 2022.
• BOE (000725.SZ, Derrick Yang): We have a relative preference for BOE, owing to
its scale advantage, more diversified revenue mix across applications and
technologies, and relatively cheap valuation vs. its Chinese peer TCL. Our PT of
Rmb5.20 is based on 1.4x 2026e P/B vs. its mid-cycle average of 1.2x since 2022,
and we think the premium is justified by its solid industry position.
• Innolux (3481.TW, Derrick Yang): We are EW on Innolux, with a price target of NT

$19.50, based on a  target multiple of 0.7x 2026e P/B (vs. mid-cycle average of 0.5x
since 2022). We think the valuation  premium implied by our target multiple over
its mid-cycle average  can be justified by the consolidation of Pioneer from
December 2025, which will likely bring its commodity revenue down to 45-50% in
2026e vs. 66% in 2024. For the FOPLP business, we think it could leverage its
existing technology expertise and legacy capacity, but more meaningful

contributions are likely to take time.
• LG Display (034220.KS, Shawn Kim): Meaningful OLED revenue contribution
should increasingly improve earnings from 2H25, but potentially weaker end-
markets keep us from turning bullish on LGD. The stock trades at 1.1x 2026e P/B
vs. a historical mid-cycle level of 0.5x.
• TCL (000100.SZ, Derrick Yang): We are EW with a price target of Rmb4.70 (1.7x
2026e P/B).  The stock is trading at 1.7x 2026e P/B vs. its mid-cycle average of 1.4x

since 2022, so we think the panel price hike cycle has been mostly baked into its
share price.
• Corning (GLW.N, Meta Marshall): GLW's display business operates efficiently and
sees further margin support  from additional currency-related price increases, while
the optical business resumes growth as carrier fiber deployments pick up. GLW's
display business closely correlates with panel maker utilization, and is its largest
earnings contributor.
• Greater China DDIC supply chain implications – Novatek (3034.TW, Daniel Yen):

Daniel's UW thesis is based on  weaker PC, smartphone and auto demand, gross
margin pressure and lack of new growth drivers. That said, Novatek's OLED DDI
roadmap looks more competitive than that of its key Korean competitors on
performance and cost, helping  it increase its market share in Apple's iPhone supply
chain.

Morgan Stanley Research 7
Where Could We Be Wrong?
• Stronger end-demand: If end-demand for major applications turns out to be
better than expected, there could be  stronger panel shipments and less panel price
pressure.
• More stringent  output control: If major panel makers collectively adjust
utilization to an extreme extent, they could mitigate pricing pressure in the case of
softer demand.
• Non-commodity display business: If the non-commodity business progresses

faster than expected, panel makers could show better financial results.

### Page 1

M
Update
Advantech  | Asia Pacific
May Sales -6% MoM/+32% YoY
In this report, we focus on Advantech's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price.
Details:
• May sales were NT$7,734mn (-6% MoM/+32% YoY).
• QTD revenue reached 75% of consensus at NT$21,274mn (+4% QoQ/+19%
YoY) and 77% of the company's guidance at NT$20,790mn (+2% QoQ/+17%
YoY at mid-point).
Our view:
• We see 2Q26 revenue tracking ahead of market expectations.

• Management sees limited GM impact from elevated component costs, as it
remains confident in passing most incremental costs to customers  (
see our
report).
• A solid B/B ratio continues to point to a strong revenue outlook, supported
by demand from the semi equipment, robotics, and smart manufacturing
segments.
• We remain constructive on Advantech, as it is well positioned to benefit
from accelerating IoT market growth amid edge AI proliferation.

Exhibit 1: Advantech's monthly sales vs. its share price since 2010

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

08 Jun 2026 14:04:50 ET │ 14 pages
Gold Circuit Electronics (2368.TW)
Record monthly sales backed by robust demand; Buy
CITI'S TAKE
Gold Circuit saw strong April–May sales driven by a better product mix (i.e.,
networking), higher ASP , and likely some new capacity ramp, in our view. We
expect stronger sales momentum in the coming months given the start of
production ramp for its existing ASIC customer. Given its market share gain
in 2026, we expect GCE’s plants to be fully utilized in the coming quarters.

Further, the company sees a high likelihood of supplying a new ASIC
customer, which we view as an important growth driver for 2027. We believe
GCE’s aggressive capacity expansion will help it capture more AI demand.
Reiterate Buy with higher TP of NT$1,740 (26x 2027E EPS).
Strong sales uptick— GCE reported NT$8.8bn sales in May (+88% YoY, +20% MoM),
driven in our view by ASP hike, enhanced product mix, and some sales delayed from
April. Combined April and May sales account for c.70% of our 2Q26 Citi/consensus

forecasts. Going forward, we believe the company could fully reflect the CCL price
hike on end-customers given its leading PCB player position and tight capacity.
ASIC demand ramping up; potential new customer from 4Q26 — We expect
existing ASIC demand to start ramping up in late 2Q26, with more meaningful
contribution from 3Q26. GCE will likely leverage its Thailand plant to support the
ASIC demand. We expect the company to gain market share at its existing ASIC

customer. Management also sees a high likelihood of securing a new AI ASIC
customer starting 4Q26. The company could supply its AI ASIC mainboard with
> 30L layer count design.
Continued capacity ramp to catch up with demand — This year, GCE is taking
measures to debottleneck and ramp up additional capacity in Taiwan and Thailand.
We also see it aiming to ramp up with one greenfield plant each year in 2027 /28/29E
across Thailand, China, and Taiwan. Despite the three plants currently planned,

management thinks overall capacity is still not able to fulfill customer demand.
Earnings/TP upward revisions; higher TP NT$1,7 40 — We raise our 2026/27 /28E
earnings by 4%/4%/6% to reflect strong sales and enhanced product mix. We raise
our TP to NT$1,740 (26x 2027E EPS) from NT$1,650 (26x 2027E EPS). We believe the
high PE multiple of 26x is justified by GCE’s market share gain with its existing ASIC
customer and potential to secure a new ASIC customer. Reiterate Buy.

Buy

### Page 5

Gold Circuit Electronics (2368.TW)
09 June 2026 Citi Research
5
Bull/Bear: Gold Circuit Electronics (2368.TW)

### Page 6

Gold Circuit Electronics (2368.TW)
09 June 2026 Citi Research
6
Gold Circuit Electronics
Company description
Founded in 1981, Gold Circuit Electronics (GCE) is a Taiwanese company
specializing in the production of printed circuit board (PCB) including double-
sided PCB, multi-layer PCB, and HDI. GCE’s products primarily focus on
applications of servers, networking, laptops, base stations, mobile phones,
and tablets. The company is known for its high-layer count design

capabilities (maximum layer count: +50L) in the PCB industry, which help it to
penetrate into more high-end applications such as AI servers and 400G
networking. Currently, server/networking accounts for +70% of its total sales.
The company has four plants, with one located in Taiwan and the other three
in China, and plans to build one plant in Thailand.
Investment strategy
We rate GCE a Buy as we believe its high-tech capabilities in
server/networking design will allow it to catch more AI opportunities from US

CSPs or US IC designers in the AI boom cycle. We expect its rising sales
exposure toward high-end applications with better margins and improving
UTR to drive its margin going forward. In our view, geopolitical tensions
should benefit non-China suppliers more, which would prevent GCE from
fierce ASP competition from China PCB peers.
Valuation
Our TP of NT$1,740 for GCE is based on 26x our 2027E EPS estimate, which is
higher than 22x or +2std level of its 1-year-forward PE over the past three

years. We believe the 26x multiple is justified by GCE's high AI
server/networking sales exposure, market share gain in existing ASIC
customer and new AI ASIC customer wins from 4Q26, which drives its AI ASIC
sales growth and margin profile. Recall that TSMC expects close to a 50%+
CAGR in the next five years for its AI-related demand, which is stronger than
any other applications. Thus, we believe investors would be willing to pay for
high-growth AI names (especially ASIC) with high PE multiples. Our target

price is equivalent to 44x/26x our 2026/27E EPS estimates and 16.7x/10.2x
our 2026/27E BVPS.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target
price include: 1) weaker-than-expected server recovery; 2) slower ramp-up
pace of new server platform; 3) less-than-expected AI demand; 4) more PCB
peers added into AI supply chain; and 5) stricter practice of China+1 by
customers due to geopolitical concerns.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.

### Page 9

Gold Circuit Electronics (2368.TW)
09 June 2026 Citi Research
9
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment
rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered

stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating
and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also

suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price
chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in

exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the
expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility

or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:
Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside

call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is
moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day

period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be
different from and does not affect a stock’s fundamental equity rating, which reflects a longer-term total absolute return

Chenbro  | Asia Pacific
May Sales +22% MoM/+37%
YoY
In this report, we focus on Chenbro's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price.
Details:
• May sales were NT$2,517mn (+22% MoM/+37% YoY).
• QTD sales reached 60% of our 2Q26 estimate at NT$7,596mn (+7% QoQ/
+40% YoY) and 59% of consensus at NT$7,736mn (+9% QoQ/+42% YoY).
Our view:
• 2Q26 revenue is tracking largely in line with our expectations.
• Management remains optimistic on the 2H26 outlook, supported by GPU/

ASIC and general purpose servers, as well as increasing rack shipments.
• Chenbro is also tapping into more rack-level mechanical components and
integration services, by leveraging its expertise in the chassis business.
• We maintain the view that Chenbro will benefit from AI server shipment
growth and its share gains, as well as the incremental contribution from rack
offerings in the coming years.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Chenbro (8210.TW)
Base case, derived from 30x 2027e P/E. We view our target multiple as justified by the 33%
earnings CAGR we see for 2025-28, which implies a PEG of 0.9. This is in line the average of
0.9 over 2014-24, when the stock was trading at an average P/E of 12x for a 14% earnings
CAGR.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger AI capex outlook
n Larger market share gains within existing customers

n Faster penetration into new customers
n Higher entry barrier among peers that leads to higher ASP/profitability
n Better macro outlook
Risks to Downside
n Softer AI capex
n Less share gains within existing customers
n Slower penetration into new customers
n Intensifying competition leading to lower ASP/profitability
n Continuous macro uncertainties

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Update
Yageo Corp.  | Asia Pacific
May sales up 7% m/m, 47% y/y
Pricing adjustments starting to show in 2Q: At our Asia AI Summit in late May
2026 (link), Yageo management stated that there were not a lot of price
adjustments baked into 1Q numbers, but there should be more to come.
At our Asia AI Summit, Yageo also mentioned that its 2Q utilization rate is
running above its guidance on the 1Q earnings call in April: Standard is running at
75-80% utilization and specialty is now at 85-90%. We believe there will be more

price hikes to come, especially for midrange/low-end MLCCs, as we see lead-times
extending and ODM/distributors building inventory. We expect pricing for tantalum
capacitors to rise as well, which we highlighted here
, in view of both higher raw
material costs and strong demand from servers.
Stay OW: The stock does not look expensive to us at 31x 2027e and 23x 2028e P/E,
vs. its Japanese and Korean peers at 40x+.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+
Howard Kao

Key Takeaways
Yageo reported May sales of NT$15.1bn (+7% m/m, +47% y/y), 3% above our
estimate.
We believe the monthly revenue beat in May was a function of both price hikes
and stronger demand.
Utilization rate is also now running above expectations.
Yageo's unaudited April + May revenues account for 69%  of our 2Q26 revenue
estimate and 70% of the Street's.

This implies upside to both our estimate and consensus.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
Power Solutions – Our
Thoughts on Rumored 800V

DC Delay
On June 9, SemiAnalysis reported that Nvidia’s (covered by Joseph Moore) planned
large-scale shipments of native 800V DC power architecture have been pushed
back to 2028, later than market expectations, while +-400V remains on schedule
for hyperscaler's ASIC deployments. Nvidia has reportedly denied the reports
.
Our take: This news is contrary to our supply chain checks at Computex. As we
stated in our Computex read-through note, NVDA at GTC Taipei indicated 800 VDC

developments are ongoing with 800 VDC power rack being ready for mass
production in 3Q26. +-400 VDC development has been redirected to 800 VDC
focus across various hyperscalers. (Exhibit 1 ).
We also note Delta will likely be the first vendor to mass produce 800 VDC
standalone power racks in 4Q26 to one of major hyperscalers in the US. We
understand the initial shipment volume won't be enormous and it will take some
time for 800 VDC protection device development and industrial safety standards to

be defined by UL and major regulations. However, 800 VDC developments remains
intact, in our view.
Exhibit
1: 800 VDC equipment readiness
Source: Nvidia, Morgan Stanley Research.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

M
Update
Compal Electronics  | Asia Pacific
May sales down 2% m/m, but
up 22% Y/Y
Our view:
• Unaudited April + May sales are tracking slightly behind our and consensus
estimates for 2Q26, primarily due to slower PC and consumer electronics
shipments.
• May notebook shipments came in 5% below our estimate at 2.0M units
(+11% m/m, -17% y/y).
• Compal's guidance continues to be that its notebook shipments will grow in
the low-single-digits q/q and revenue will grow in the double-digits q/q in

2Q26.
• We  believe the continued pull-in of consumer electronic devices in 1H26 will
lead to a more sub-seasonal 2H26.
• On our estimates, Compal is trading at ~17x 2027e P/E, above its past five-
year P/E average of ~14x.
• We stay UW on Compal.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Compal Electronics (2324.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Our key assumptions include a cost of equity of 9.1% (beta
1.3, equity risk premium 6.0%, and risk-free rate 1.5%), a medium-term growth rate of 5.0%,
and a long-term earnings growth rate of 3.0%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected NB and smartphone demand
n Further share gains in iPad
n Margin expansion thanks to better scale

Risks to Downside
n Weaker-than-expected NB demand
n Margin contraction from sales shortfall and fierce price competition
n Weaker-than-expected iPad shipments

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Thoughts on Investor Concerns
Regarding CPO
Long-term story unchanged, while sentiment might be weak in
the near term to reset expectations.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+
Tiffany Yeh
Equity Analyst
Tiffany.Yeh@morganstanley.com                                               +886 2 7712-3032
Charlie Chan
Equity Analyst
Charlie.Chan@morganstanley.com                                               +886 2 2730-1725

Greater China Technology Semiconductors
Asia Pacific
Industry View Attractive
What's new? Investors' feedback shows they are worried about delays in CPO
introduction, driving further sell-down in CPO-related names.
Our thoughts: In our CPO update, we estimate 2027 optical engine (OE) shipments
likely at 6-7mn units, including scale-up and scale-out solutions. Although TSMC
decided to expand PIC capacity to 10kwpm in 1Q27, SoIC yield (50-60%) and
downstream assembly yield (20-50%) remain the key swing factors for final

shipments. However, investors' expectations for OE sit at 20-30mn units, likely
resetting near-term CPO stock sentiment. On the other hand, we agree 2026-28
would be a cadence where transceivers, optics (e.g., CPO/NPO) and copper co-exist,
as the mainstream solution still stays at 1.6/3.2T and CPO still needs time to ramp,
with CPO starting to boom in 2028 onward (
see note).
Stock implication: We stay OW on key CPO enablers – TSMC (2330.TW), ASE

(3711.TW), FOCI (3363.TWO), AllRing (6187.TWO), MPI (6223.TWO), Hon Precision
(7769.TW) and Winway (6515.TW) – as the long-term CPO story remains unchanged.
FOCI's CPO revenue should start to ramp in late 3Q; revenue before then should
remain lukewarm as it prepares for mass production. For Suzhou TFC Optical
(300394.SZ), while the company has a good opportunity to play an important role
in CPO components; covering analyst Andy Meng EW on   the stock, as the company

also has material exposure to pluggable transceiver components business but at a
significant valuation premium to A-share industry peers.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley
Research as only a single factor in making their investment

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
Modeling 2027 optical engine shipments at 6-7mn units suggests a far smaller
ramp than investor expectations of 20-30mn units.
Linking capacity and output, PIC capacity expansion to 10kwpm in 1Q27 still
leaves SoIC yield at 50–60% as a key swing factor.
Stressing manufacturing bottlenecks, downstream assembly yield at 20-50%

now sets another major swing factor for final CPO-related shipments.
Positioning the inflection, CPO starting to boom from 2028 onward should
support a longer-dated catalyst path despite near-term resets in expectations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Company Report 
公司研究報告 
本報告僅供宏遠投顧內部及客戶參考，雖已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場
或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保
證。非經本公司同意，不得將本報告加以引用或轉載

1 
3596智易(Arcadyan Co.)

報 告 日 期 ：2026/06/08 研 究 員： 方姿婷  (Lesley  Fang ) 
壹、投 資 建 議

一. 智易為網通設備專業廠商，近 5 年營收 14%CAGR  
智易 (3596) 為 仁 寶 集 團 旗 下 專 業 網 通 設 備 製 造 廠 ， 總 部 設 立 於 新
竹，在 昆 山、上 海、美 國 與 歐 洲 設 有 據 點，2026 年 智 易 產 品 別Smart 
Home Solution 佔 26%、Broadband 佔 31%、Mobility Solution&&Others
佔 43%， 客 戶 組 成 主 要 為 歐 美 電 信 營 運 商 、 有 線 頻 道 業 者 、 系 統
整 合 業 者(Service Provider) 等 。 公 司 競 爭 優 勢 為 ， 深 耕 歐 美 主 要 電
信 級 客 戶 ， 借 重 母 公 司 仁 寶 集 團 資 源 挹 注 穩 健 成 長 動 能 ， 例 如 ：
追 料 、 共 用 料 號 、 廠 區 調 度 等 協 助 。 產 能 區 域 比重 中 國 昆 山 地 區
佔 30%、 越 南廠佔 70% ，增 加 越 南 新 產 能， 以 因 應 新 興 市 場 寬 頻
升 級 需 求 ， 也 避 免 於 中 國 產 能 過 於 集 中 之 營 運 風 險 ， 長 期 而 言 台
系 網 通 廠 可 望 受 惠 轉 單 效 應，2025 ~2026 年間 ，智 易指 出 當 前積極
尋 求 南 亞 地 區 外 包 產 能 與 新 建 廠 房 之 計 劃。

二. 隨著 產品組合持續轉佳提升，毛利率顯著提升中  
北 美 、 歐 洲 市 場 升 級Docsis 3.1 Cable  Modem (預期 2026 年 升 級 至
Docsis 4.0) 與 Wireless Router ，將 持 續 為 業 者 帶 來 產 品 升 級 的 趨 勢 。
智 易 隨 著 越 南 新 廠 產 能 稼 動 率 逐 季 提 升 ， 拉 丁 美 洲 、 東 南 亞 電 信
商 CPE 出 貨 量 增，展 望2026 年 智 易 預 期Smart Home Gateway 、ioT
在 歐 洲 市 場、美 洲 市 場、新 興 市 場 市 佔 率 進 一 步 提 升，除 傳 統CPE
以 外，5G 相關 CPE 產 品，陸 續 獲 得 各 大 區 域 電 信 系 統 業 者(歐洲 D
電 信 公 司 ； 北 美A、V 電 信 公 司)認 證 ， 可 望 帶 動 營 收 成 長 動 能 與
規 模 經 濟 效 應 ， 帶 動 營 業 毛 利 率 逐 季 轉 佳 。 此 外 ， 在 軟 硬 整 合 技
術 能 力 上 ， 智 易 也 與 電 信 商 共 同 合 作 開 發 平 台 ， 進 軍 歐 系 電 信 商
一 級 供 應 滲 透 率 達70% ， 都 是 智 易 近 年 來 獲 利 穩 健 的 關 鍵 。2022
年~2027  年 機 構 預 估 5G FWA 連 結 數 成 長 至 7,000~8,000 萬 ， 其
中 5G FWA 初 期 預 計 滲 透 率 為 15~20% 約達  1,200~1,600 萬 組 ，
智 易 認 為 憑 藉 自 身 軟 硬 體 整 合 能 力 ，回 顧 過 往 5G FWA 對 於 智 易
2023 年 營 收 貢 獻 達30~40% 之間 (因 5G FWA 案 量 包 含 軟 硬 體 整 合 銷
售 單 價 較 高)。展望 2026 年 智 易 地5G FWA 在 北 美 市 場 三 大 電 信 營
運 商 主 導 下，智 易 預 期5G FWA 裝 置 數 量 倍 數 成長，帶 動 智 易 5G 
FWA 業務於 2026 年仍可 維 持 高 度 成 長， 也 可 抵 消Smart Home 當
中的 STB 因 為 記 憶 體 成 本 高 漲 而 遭 受 訂 單 調 整 之 影 響。

三. 網通 CPE 升級至 WiFi -7 於 2026 年起放量毛利率佳  
根據  Gartner 預 估，Wi-Fi 6 企 業 與 中 小 型 商 務 用 戶 產 值 將 從2019
投資評等：買進 
目標價位：220.0 元 
      15.00X 2026 EPS

     12.73X 2026 EPS

四.投資建議：買進，目標價 220 元 
智 易 公 司 競 爭 優 勢 為 ， 深 耕 歐 美 主 要 電 信 級 客 戶 ， 借 重 母 公 司 仁
寶 集 團 資 源 挹 注 穩 健 成 長 動 能 ， 且 透 過 與 客 戶 共 同 開 發 軟 體 加 值
優 化 其 IAD 產 品 之 附 加 價 值。預估 2026 年，智 易 年 營 收 為565.24
億 元，+6.69%YoY ，毛 利 率 為 15.49% ，稅 後 淨 利 為33.00 億 元，稅
後 EPS+14.97 元。 智易 陸 續 受 惠 WiFi-7 家 用 終 端 與 企 業 級機房用
新 品 拉 貨 潮，重 回 成 長 軌 道，此 外，智 易 於 2025 ~2026 年起 看 待 歐
洲 市 場 經 濟 復 甦， 營 運 優 於 市 場 預 期， 且 受 惠北美 5G FWA 拉貨
動 能 重 啟，給 予 投 資 建 議 為 買 進，目標價 220 元(15 .00x 20 26 EPS)。

竹 ， 在 昆 山 、 上 海 、 美 國 與 歐 洲 設 有 據 點 。 產 品 包 含 寬 頻 網 路 產
品(涵蓋 IAD 、IP STB 、PON 、xDSL 、Femtocell 等)、AP/Gateway 產

二.25 年營運概況 及 26 年展望  
1. 智 易 產 品 以Home Gateway 與 Fixed Mobile 為 主 軸 
智 易 預 期 Gateway 、ioT 在 歐 洲 市 場 、 美 洲 市 場 、 新 興 市 場 市 佔 率
進 一 步 提 升，除 傳 統CPE 以 外，5G 相關 CPE 產 品，陸 續 獲 得 各 大
區 域 電 信 系 統 業 者(歐洲 D 電 信 公 司；北 美A、V 電 信 公 司)認 證 ，
可 望 帶 動2022 年 營 收 成 長 動 能 與 規 模 經 濟 效 應，帶 動 營 業 毛 利 率
逐 季 轉 佳 。

2. 未來 2~3 年為 WiFi -7 換 機 潮 ， 企 業 端 產 品 汰 換 先 行 放 量 
隨 著 串 流 影 音 與XR( 虛 擬 實 境 技 術)逐 漸 成 熟 ， 使 用 者 對 於 無 線 高
頻 寬 傳 輸 要 求 殷 切，帶 動 了802.11ad 、802.11ax 、Mesh 、5G 等 各 種
高 頻 寬 無 線 通 訊 技 術 快 速 成 長 。 未 來 十 年 ，5G 是 開 啟 移 動 終 端 與
萬 物 互 聯 之 技 術 開 端，市 場 預 期2010 年 全 球 有53 億 移 動 終 端，到
2020 年 達 到 107 億，至 2030 年 成 長 到170 億 ， 尤 其 是 中 國 市 場 移
動 用 戶 月 流 量 將 持 續 大 幅 增 長 至2030 年達 130 倍 之 高 ， 所 帶 來 的
經 濟 規 模 十 分 巨 大。展望 2025 年，智 易 指 出Smart Home 產 品 線 中
搭載 WiFi -7 比重 持續提升 ，顯 示 WiFi -7 升 級 對 於 網 通 產 品 升 級 有
利，隨著網通 WiFi 晶片價格日漸降至終端裝置搭載之甜蜜點 ，
WiFi -7 在 相 對 應 的 頻 段 及 多 功 能 傳 輸 下 ， 單 一 裝 置 WiFi 
FEM(Front -End Module) 數 量 從 上 一 代WiFi -6 的 10~12 顆， WiFi -6
天 線 模 組 從6~8 組，逐 步 升 級 至WiFi -7 預 期 呈 現 倍 數 增 長，WiFi -7
天 線 模 組 國 內 網 通 廠 啟 碁 、 中 磊 、 智 易 等 皆 為 相 關 系 統 整 合 廠 ，
預期 WiFi -7 規 格 於 2025 年將 展 開 升 級。

- 1 - 
個股報告 
復盛(6670) 
2026 年營收及獲利重回成長 
投資評等 目標價 
逢低買進 $338 
前次投資建議 2023.04.14： 
「區間操作」 ，目標價238元

報告日期：2026.06.12 研 究 員：鄭師安

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ 受惠新客戶訂單及漲價效應，公司營收及獲利重回成長，投資評等為「逢低買進」，6 個月
目標價 338 元(12X 26(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間 20%。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/06/11 收盤價： 282 ➢ 公司 26Q1 營收 84.69 億元，YoY-0.1%，毛利率 24%，較
去年同期下滑約 2.1%，稅後淨利 9.7 億元，YoY-20.4%，
EPS 6.96 元。26Q1 受匯率、材料成本、關稅及產品組合
等因素影響，營收及獲利不如預期。另 26Q1 越南廠區有
擴大生產，使折舊攤提明顯提升。 
➢ 大客戶在其 26Q1 法說會中均表示今年展望樂觀，公司
26Q1 毛利率低於預期，惟 4、5 月自結毛利率已比 26Q1
好，26Q2 毛利率有機會比 26Q1 多 2%。另希望美國關稅
可退稅，有跟客戶說希望返還部分退稅給公司，把一些之
前多分擔的成本要回來，會反映在未來的客戶報價上。 
➢ 公司認為 26Q1 表現已經比預期好，26Q2及 26Q3 營收年
成長均可達 20%，呈現淡季不淡。26Q2營收挑戰 80億元，
26Q3 會往下掉一點，26Q4 會是全年最高(比去年同期好
一點)。2026 全年營收挑戰雙位數成長，主要來自新客戶
訂單，惟匯率是重要影響因素。原物料影響最大是鎢粉，
但近期有看到價格持平，甚至下降。 
➢ 其他新事業部份，為進軍手工具產業，2025 年買了伯鑫，
仍會持續併購手工具機公司，手上有幾個案子在 DD，未
來希望成為重要的事業體。另子公司力野有在考慮進軍機
器人領域，其技術可以做機器人身體。 
➢ 兆豐國際預估 2026年營收 326.9億元，YoY+9.7%，毛利
率 25.3%，稅後淨利 39.3 億元，EPS 28.18 元，公司營收
及獲利重回成長，投資評等為「逢低買進」，6 個月目標
價 338元(12X 26(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間 20%。

營 運 分 析

公司為全球高爾夫球頭代工龍頭，每年維持高獲利 
公司成立於 1953 年，以空壓機起家，1978 年跨入高爾夫球桿頭代工。組織
再造後，將集團事業部分割為 3 家公司，其中高爾夫代工業務獨立為復盛應
用，主要核心技術在於高爾夫球桿頭之開模、精密拖拉鑄造、鍛造 in house
產能等。

公司高爾夫球主要客戶遍及美國及日本知名品牌，包含 Callaway、Cobra、
Ping 等；航太產品主要客戶為 Eaton、Meggitt；曲棍球及棒球主要客戶為
Alpinestars、Bauer、Eaton。公司 2021 年前五大客戶均屬高爾夫球產業，
營收比重約 71%。26Q1 各產品營收比重：高爾夫球頭 61%、桿身及套裝品 
11%、球桿頭配件 9%、航太 5%、滑冰靴及越野車靴及保護裝備 (民盛應用) 
8%。

- 3 - 
個股報告 
受多重因素影響，26Q1營收及獲利不如預期 
公司 26Q1 營收 84.69 億元，YoY-0.1%，毛利率 24%，較去年同期下滑約
2.1%，亦較 25Q4下滑約 2.8%，稅後淨利 9.7 億元，YoY-20.4%，EPS 6.96
元。26Q1 受匯率、材料成本、關稅及產品組合等因素影響，營收及獲利不
如預期。另 26Q1 越南廠區有擴大生產，使折舊攤提明顯提升。

大客戶在其 26Q1 法說會中均表示今年展望樂觀，公司 26Q1 毛利率低於預
期，惟 4、5月自結毛利率已比 26Q1好，26Q2毛利率有機會比 26Q1 多 2%。
另希望美國關稅可退稅，有跟客戶說希望返還部分退稅給公司，把一些之前
多分擔的成本要回來，會反映在未來的客戶報價上。

公司認為 26Q1表現已經比預期好，26Q2及 26Q3營收年成長均可達 20%，
呈現淡季不淡。26Q2 營收挑戰 80 億元，26Q3 會往下掉一點，26Q4 會是
全年最高(比去年同期好一點)。2026 全年營收挑戰雙位數成長，主要來自新
客戶訂單，惟匯率是重要影響因素。原物料影響最大是鎢粉，但近期有看到
價格持平，甚至下降。

圖二、26Q1 美國整體高爾夫下場打球回合數比去年高

- 4 - 
個股報告

今年資本支出約 55億元，其中包含 22億的併購預算。資本支出較去年多是
因為越南有多買一塊地 ，需進行去瓶頸產能建置 。另公司股利配發率約
60~70%，未來亦同。

公司持續拓展新事業，為中長期成長奠定基礎 
子公司民盛於 2026/03/25 上櫃，其 60%營收來自腳部相關，30~35%來自腳
以上相關產品，26Q1 營收 6.7 億元，YoY-18.2%，第一季主要是曲棍球第
一大客戶出貨旺季，但今年新產品良率沒有拉上來，使出貨不順進而影響營
收及獲利。26q2 良率有回，加上在手訂單未流失只是遞延，預期營收及獲利
可望逐季回穩。

民盛 26Q1產品比重為：曲棍球 40.8%、摩托車 30.3%、棒球 17.9%及其他
10.9%。26Q1是全年最辛苦的一季，目前訂單回穩，能見度看到 26Q3，今
年營收逐季往上，毛利率維持往年水準。價格部分，部分客戶同意價格調漲，
26Q3 單月營收有機會挑戰 1,000 萬美元。今年資本支出約 0.8 億元，主要
偏生產設備，每年折舊約 0.8~0.9 億元，目前產能接近滿載。因原物料而漲
價的效應預計 26Q3 反映，近期看到蠻多急單，這些訂單預計 26Q3 出貨。

其他新事業部份，為進軍手工具產業，2025年買了伯鑫，仍會持續併購手工
具機公司，手上有幾個案子在 DD，未來希望成為重要的事業體。台灣手工
具機產值 900 億元，有兩三百家公司在做；高爾夫產值 400 億元，僅 6家公
司在做。另子公司力野有在考慮進軍機器人領域，其技術可以做機器人身體。
